2.5 截断-F1 相关性分析\\n    truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    non_truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if not r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    if truncated_f1s and non_truncated_f1s:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s)\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s)\\n        gap = nt_mean - t_mean\\n    else:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s) if truncated_f1s else None\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s) if non_truncated_f1s else None\\n        gap = None\\n    truncation_analysis = {\\n        \\\"truncated_count\\\": len(truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"non_truncated_count\\\": len(non_truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(t_mean, 4) if t_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"non_truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(nt_mean, 4) if nt_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"f1_gap\\\": round(gap, 4) if gap is not None else None,\\n        \\\"gap_significant\\\": abs(gap) > 0.1 if gap is not None else False,\\n    }\\n    logger.info(\\\"Truncation analysis: trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), non-trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), gap=%.3f\\\",\\n                len(truncated_f1s), t_mean or 0, len(non_truncated_f1s), nt_mean or 0, gap or 0)\\n\\n    # 3. 筛选\\n    selected, filter_stats = filter_records(scored, args)\\n\\n    # 4. 转换为 SFT 训练格式\\n    sft_data = format_sft_training_data(selected)\\n    train_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_train.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(train_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for item in sft_data:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training data → %s (%d samples)\\\", train_path, len(sft_data))\\n\\n    # 1. 加载生成结果\\n    records = load_generations(args.input_dir)\\n\\n    # 2. 加载 CED 模型并打分\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading NLI model: %s\\\", args.nli_model_path)\\n    from freige.rewards.ced_reward import CEDRewardModel\\n    ced_model = CEDRewardModel(model_name=args.nli_model_path, device=args.nli_device)\\n\\n    scored = score_all_records(records, ced_model, tau=args.tau, format_weight=args.format_weight, f1_weight=args.f1_weight, ced_weight=args.ced_weight)\\n\\n    # 保存带分数的完整记录\\n    scores_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_scores.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(scores_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for rec in scored:\\n            # 不��存大字段以节省空间\\n            slim = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in (\\\"prompt\\\", \\\"sents\\\", \\\"gold_triples\\\")}\\n            f.write(json.dumps(slim, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Scores → %s\\\", scores_path)\\n\\n    # 2.5 截断-F1 相关性分析\\n    truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    non_truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if not r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    if truncated_f1s and non_truncated_f1s:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s)\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s)\\n        gap = nt_mean - t_mean\\n    else:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s) if truncated_f1s else None\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s) if non_truncated_f1s else None\\n        gap = None\\n    truncation_analysis = {\\n        \\\"truncated_count\\\": len(truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"non_truncated_count\\\": len(non_truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(t_mean, 4) if t_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"non_truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(nt_mean, 4) if nt_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"f1_gap\\\": round(gap, 4) if gap is not None else None,\\n        \\\"gap_significant\\\": abs(gap) > 0.1 if gap is not None else False,\\n    }\\n    logger.info(\\\"Truncation analysis: trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), non-trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), gap=%.3f\\\",\\n                len(truncated_f1s), t_mean or 0, len(non_truncated_f1s), nt_mean or 0, gap or 0)\\n\\n    # 3. 筛选\\n    selected, filter_stats = filter_records(scored, args)\\n\\n    # 4. 转换为 SFT 训练格式\\n    sft_data = format_sft_training_data(selected)\\n    train_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_train.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(train_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for item in sft_data:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training data → %s (%d samples)\\\", train_path, len(sft_data))\\n\\n    # 5. 统计报告\\n    report = generate_report(scored, selected)\\n    report[\\\"f1_filter_stats\\\"] = filter_stats\\n    report[\\\"truncation_analysis\\\"] = truncation_analysis\\n    report[\\\"args\\\"] = vars(args)\\n    report_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_report.json\\\"\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Report → %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台摘要\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"  Total records:    {report['total_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selected:         {report['selected_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selection rate:   {report['selection_rate']:.1%}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Unique docs:      {report['unique_docs_selected']}/{report['unique_docs_total']}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\\n    for name in [\\\"format_reward\\\", \\\"f1_reward\\\", \\\"ced_reward\\\", \\\"combined_score\\\"]:\\n        stats = report[f\\\"{name}_stats\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"  {name:20s}  mean={stats['mean']:.3f}  std={stats['std']:.3f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"median={stats['median']:.3f}  [min={stats['min']:.3f}, max={stats['max']:.3f}]\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.38}}"

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"推理+评测完整流程: 加载 Qwen3-4B + LoRA adapter → 批量推理 → 解析输出 → 计算 F1/Evi-F1/EDCR。\\n\\n输入:\\n  - base model (Qwen3-4B) + 可选 LoRA adapter (SFT/GRPO checkpoint)\\n  - DocRED JSON 数据目录 (含 dev.json, train_annotated.json)\\n\\n输出 (保存到 --output_dir):\\n  - metrics.json: F1, Ign-F1, Evi-F1, EDCR, format_compliance_rate, n_truncated 等\\n  - predictions.json: 每个文档的详细预测 (doc_id, raw_output, parsed_triples, gold_triples, truncated, format_ok)\\n\\n依赖: transformers, peft, bitsandbytes, torch, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom peft import PeftModel\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nfrom freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor, SPLIT_FILENAMES\\nfrom freige.eval.evaluator import DocREDEvaluator, gold_from_docred, parse_model_output\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# A. 模型加载\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef model 路径\\n        adapter_path: LoRA adapter 路径（SFT 或 GRPO checkpoint），None 则不加载\\n    Returns:\\n        (model, tokenizer)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer: %s\\\", model_path)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        padding_side=\\\"left\\\",\\n    )\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is model_kwargs[\\\"torch_dtype\\\"] = torch.bfloat16\\n\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_path, **model_kwargs)\\n\\n    if sft_adapter_path:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading & merging SFT adapter: %s\\\", sft_adapter_path)\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, sft_adapter_path)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n\\n    if adapter_path:\\n [截断: 输出过长，省略 5467 字符] ...\\n\\nrse.BooleanOptionalAction,\\n                        default=True, help=\\\"4-bit NF4 quantization（default: on）\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\\n        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(args.seed)\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    # 自动检测 model_path 是 adapter 还是完整模型\\n    adapter_config = Path(args.model_path) / \\\"adapter_config.json\\\"\\n    if adapter_config.exists():\\n        base_model_path = args.base_model\\n        adapter_path = args.model_path\\n        logger.info(\\\"Detected adapter at %s, base: %s\\\", adapter_path, base_model_path)\\n    else:\\n        base_model_path = args.model_path\\n        adapter_path = None\\n        logger.info(\\\"Full model: %s (no adapter)\\\", base_model_path)\\n\\n    model, tokenizer = load_model_and_tokenizer(\\n        base_model_path, adapter_path, sft_adapter_path=args.sft_adapter,\\n        quantize=args.quantize,\\n    )\\n    eval_items, gold, gold_by_doc = prepare_eval_data(\\n        args.data_path, args.split, tokenizer\\n    )\\n\\n    t0 = time.time()\\n    raw_results = run_inference(\\n        model, tokenizer, eval_items, args.batch_size, args.max_new_tokens\\n    )\\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n    logger.info(\\\"Inference: %.1fs (%.2f s/doc)\\\", elapsed, elapsed / max(len(eval_items), 1))\\n\\n    # 展平为 evaluator 格式\\n    all_predictions = []\\n    raw_outputs_for_eval = []\\n    for r in raw_results:\\n        doc_id = r[\\\"doc_id\\\"]\\n        for triple in r[\\\"parsed_triples\\\"]:\\n            all_predictions.append({\\n                \\\"doc_id\\\": doc_id,\\n                \\\"head\\\": triple[\\\"head\\\"],\\n                \\\"tail\\\": triple[\\\"tail\\\"],\\n                \\\"relation\\\": triple[\\\"relation\\\"],\\n                \\\"evidence\\\": triple.get(\\\"evidence\\\", []),\\n            })\\n        raw_outputs_for_eval.append({\\\"doc_id\\\": doc_id, \\\"raw_text\\\": r[\\\"raw_output\\\"]})\\n\\n    metrics = evaluate(all_predictions, gold, raw_outputs_for_eval, args.data_path, args.split)\\n\\n    n_truncated = sum(1 for r in raw_results if r[\\\"truncated\\\"])\\n    n_format_ok = sum(1 for r in raw_results if r[\\\"format_ok\\\"])\\n    n_total = len(raw_results)\\n    metrics[\\\"n_documents\\\"] = n_total\\n    metrics[\\\"n_predictions\\\"] = len(all_predictions)\\n    metrics[\\\"n_truncated\\\"] = n_truncated\\n    metrics[\\\"n_format_ok\\\"] = n_format_ok\\n    metrics[\\\"format_ok_rate\\\"] = n_format_ok / n_total if n_total else 0.0\\n    metrics[\\\"inference_time_seconds\\\"] = round(elapsed, 1)\\n    metrics[\\\"config\\\"] = vars(args)\\n\\n    # 保存 metrics.json\\n    metrics_path = output_dir / \\\"metrics.json\\\"\\n    with open(metrics_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Metrics → %s\\\", metrics_path)\\n\\n    # 保存 predictions.json（每文档详细信息）\\n    detailed = []\\n    for r in raw_results:\\n        doc_id = r[\\\"doc_id\\\"]\\n        doc_gold = gold_by_doc.get(doc_id, [])\\n        gold_triples = [\\n            {\\\"head\\\": g[\\\"head\\\"], \\\"tail\\\": g[\\\"tail\\\"], \\\"relation\\\": g[\\\"relation\\\"],\\n             \\\"evidence\\\": g.get(\\\"evidence\\\", [])}\\n            for g in doc_gold\\n        ]\\n        detailed.append({\\n            \\\"doc_id\\\": doc_id,\\n            \\\"raw_output\\\": r[\\\"raw_output\\\"],\\n            \\\"parsed_triples\\\": r[\\\"parsed_triples\\\"],\\n            \\\"gold_triples\\\": gold_triples,\\n            \\\"truncated\\\": r[\\\"truncated\\\"],\\n            \\\"format_ok\\\": r[\\\"format_ok\\\"],\\n        })\\n\\n    pred_path = output_dir / \\\"predictions.json\\\"\\n    with open(pred_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(detailed, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Predictions → %s\\\", pred_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台汇总\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n    print(f\\\"  F1:          {metrics['f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Ign-F1:      {metrics['ign_f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Evi-F1:      {metrics['evi_f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  EDCR:        {metrics.get('edcr', 0):.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Format OK:   {n_format_ok}/{n_total} ({metrics['format_ok_rate']:.1%})\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Truncated:   {n_truncated}/{n_total}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Predictions: {len(all_predictions)}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good, I have all the information I need. Let me also check what existing verbalize_triple function does and how CED scoring works in the rsft_score_filter context - specifically, how it gets sents and evidence for the CED reward.

Key observations:
1. NLI model is cached at `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/hub/models--cross-encoder--nli-deberta-v3-base`
2. SFT adapter at `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`
3. Base model at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`


4. Dev data at `/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/dev.json`

I can reuse several existing modules: `rsft_generate.py` has the model loading and batch generation functions, `rsft_score_filter.py` contains the CED scoring logic, `evaluator.py` has the DocRED evaluation utilities and output parsing, and `ced_reward.py` has the reward model implementation.

Now I'm realizing the key challenge with using CED as a reranker: during training, CED compares predicted evidence against hard negatives using gold triple information, but for reranking I won't have gold triples to work with. Looking at how `rsft_score_filter.py` computes CED rewards, it relies on gold triples and sentences to calculate scores for predicted triples that match gold ones. For the reranker approach, I need to figure out how to score candidate outputs without that gold information.

Actually, rethinking this—the task is to use CED scoring to select the best candidate from multiple generations. For each predicted triple with its associated evidence sentences, I can verbalize the triple as a claim and use CED to measure how well the cited evidence supports that claim, comparing it against non-cited sentences as negatives.

The reranking pipeline would be: generate N=8 candidates, compute the average CED score across all predicted triples in each candidate, then pick the one with the highest score. This is purely self-supervised—no gold labels needed—and directly measures output quality based on how coherent the model's own evidence choices are.

For comparison, I'll also implement an oracle reranker that selects based on F1 against gold triples, and a random baseline. Now I'm setting up the script, making sure to reuse existing utilities like the model loader, output parser, and CED reward model. I need to handle the Qwen3 generation config override and avoid quantization, running everything on cuda:0. The dev set should have around 998 documents based on the file size, so I'm structuring the script to iterate through those and generate candidates for each. 我需要重新设计 CED 评分方法，避免在 reranking 时使用金标签信息。对于每个候选输出，我会解析预测的三元组，将其转化为自然语言声明，然后基于预测的证据而非金标签来计算 NLI 分数。

同时，我还需要实现一个 Oracle reranker，通过计算每个候选与金标签三元组的 F1 分数来选择最优候选。

现在的问题是关于关系映射：模型输出的是自然语言关系名称（比如"head of government"），但 verbalize_triple 函数期望的是 Pxxx 代码。我需要找到一个名称到代码的映射，或者使用通用模板来处理这个转换。

从 rsft_score_filter.py 的导入来看，verbalize_triple 函数来自 ced_reward.py，它应该接收 head、relation 和 tail 参数。虽然 ced_reward.py 的输出被截断了，但我能看到 VERBALIZATION_TEMPLATES 字典的结构。关键问题是 verbalize_triple 是否能处理自然语言关系名称，或者我需要先建立一个反向映射来将自然语言名称转换为 Pxxx 代码。
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the complete picture. Let me write the script.

Key insights from code review:
1. `generate_batch` expands prompts × num_generations and generates all at once. For batch_size=4 and N=8, that's 32 sequences per batch. This might be a lot for one GPU.
2. `prepare_generation_data` returns items with `doc_id, prompt, gold_triples, sents, sft_output`
3. `generate_batch` returns `(list[list[str]], list[list[bool]])` - per-prompt results.


4. For the reranker script, I need to be careful about memory since generating 8 outputs per doc for ~998 docs is substantial. I'll process in batches and need to consider that with batch_size=4 and N=8, I'm generating 32 sequences simultaneously, which should be manageable on a 5090 with bf16 for a 4B model.

However, since DocRED documents can be quite long, the memory could get tight with larger batch sizes. Looking at the actual code structure, it processes one prompt at a time through generate_batch, so I'll stick with batch_size=1 to be safe—that keeps it to 8 sequences per call. I'm estimating the full runtime at around 20-40 minutes total—generation will take 15-30 minutes across all docs with their 8 candidates each, and CED scoring another 5-10 minutes. For the implementation, I'll process one document at a time during generation since each has different prompt lengths, and handle the CED scoring similarly since the existing model works on individual triples.

Writing the script now...
```

```json
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/freige/eval/ced_reranker.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"CED-as-reranker PoC: 推理时用 CED 从 N=8 SFT candidates 中选最佳输出。\n\n不做二次训练，纯推理时 reranking。验证 CED scoring 能否在 SFT candidates 中\n选出更好的输出，提升 rel_f1 / evi_f1 / EDCR。\n\n流程:\n  1. 加载 SFT warmup 模型 (Qwen3-4B + SFT adapter merged, bf16)\n  2. 对 dev set 每个 doc 生成 N=8 candidates (T=0.7)\n  3. CED 打分每个 candidate (用 predicted evidence, 不用 gold)\n  4. 三种选择策略: CED reranker / Oracle / Random\n  5. DocREDEvaluator 计算完整指标\n\n输入:\n  - Base model: /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B\n  - SFT adapter: /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output\n  - NLI model: cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (HF cache)\n  - Dev data: /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/dev.json\n\n输出 (/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/ced_reranker_poc/):\n  - results.json: 三种策略完整指标 + SFT baseline 对比\n  - generations.jsonl: 中间生成结果 (断点续传)\n  - per_doc_scores.jsonl: 每个 doc 的 CED/Oracle 打分\n\n依赖: freige.training.rsft_generate, freige.eval.evaluator, freige.rewards.ced_reward\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport random\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nfrom freige.data.docred_processor import DOCRED_REL_INFO\nfrom freige.eval.evaluator import DocREDEvaluator, gold_from_docred, parse_model_output\nfrom freige.rewards.ced_reward import (\n    CEDRewardModel,\n    VERBALIZATION_TEMPLATES,\n    verbalize_triple,\n)\nfrom freige.training.rsft_generate import (\n    generate_batch,\n    load_model_and_tokenizer,\n    prepare_generation_data,\n)\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# relation name → Pxxx code, for verbalization template lookup\n_REL_NAME_TO_CODE = {v.lower().strip(): k for k, v in DOCRED_REL_INFO.items()}\n\n\ndef verbalize_by_name(head: str, relation: str, tail: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Verbalize a triple using relation name (model output format) instead of Pxxx code.\"\"\"\n    pcode = _REL_NAME_TO_CODE.get(relation.lower().strip())\n    if pcode:\n        return verbalize_triple(head, pcode, tail)\n    return f\"{head} {relation} {tail}.\"\n\n\ndef score_candidate_ced(\n    parsed_triples: list[dict],\n    sents: list[str],\n    ced_model: CEDRewardModel,\n    tau: float = 0.5,\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"CED score for one candidate — uses only predicted evidence, no gold info.\n\n    For each predicted triple:\n      claim   = verbalized triple\n      pos     = concat of model-cited evidence sentences\n      neg     = all other sentences in the document\n      CED(t)  = max(0, p_pos - max_j p_neg_j) * I(p_pos > tau)\n    Returns mean CED across triples.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not parsed_triples:\n        return 0.0\n\n    n_sents = len(sents)\n    all_ids = set(range(n_sents))\n    scores = []\n\n    for t in parsed_triples:\n        if not isinstance(t, dict):\n            continue\n        head = t.get(\"head\", \"\")\n        relation = t.get(\"relation\", \"\")\n        tail = t.get(\"tail\", \"\")\n        evidence = t.get(\"evidence\", [])\n        if not (head and relation and tail):\n            continue\n\n        claim = verbalize_by_name(head, relation, tail)\n\n        evi_ids = {int(e) for e in evidence if isinstance(e, int) and 0 <= int(e) < n_sents}\n        if not evi_ids:\n            scores.append(0.0)\n            continue\n\n        cited = [sents[i] for i in sorted(evi_ids)]\n        neg_ids = all_ids - evi_ids\n        negs = [sents[i] for i in sorted(neg_ids)]\n\n        if negs:\n            r = ced_model.compute_ced_reward(claim, cited, negs, tau=tau)\n        else:\n            r = ced_model.compute_flat_nli_reward(claim, cited, tau=tau)\n        scores.append(r[\"reward\"])\n\n    return float(np.mean(scores)) if scores else 0.0\n\n\ndef candidate_rel_f1(parsed_triples: list[dict], gold_triples: list[dict]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Per-candidate relation F1 against gold (for Oracle selection).\"\"\"\n    gold_set = {\n        (str(g[\"head\"]).lower().strip(),\n         str(g[\"relation\"]).lower().strip(),\n         str(g[\"tail\"]).lower().strip())\n        for g in gold_triples\n    }\n    pred_set = set()\n    for p in parsed_triples:\n        if isinstance(p, dict):\n            pred_set.add((\n                str(p.get(\"head\", \"\")).lower().strip(),\n                str(p.get(\"relation\", \"\")).lower().strip(),\n                str(p.get(\"tail\", \"\")).lower().strip(),\n            ))\n    if not gold_set:\n        return 1.0 if not pred_set else 0.0\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\n    prec = tp / len(pred_set) if pred_set else 0.0\n    rec = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\n    return 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef evaluate_strategy(\n    selections: dict,\n    gold: list[dict],\n    train_file: str,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Run DocREDEvaluator on the selected candidates.\"\"\"\n    all_preds = []\n    for doc_id, cand in selections.items():\n        for t in cand[\"parsed_triples\"]:\n            all_preds.append({\n                \"doc_id\": doc_id,\n                \"head\": t[\"head\"],\n                \"tail\": t[\"tail\"],\n                \"relation\": t[\"relation\"],\n                \"evidence\": t.get(\"evidence\", []),\n            })\n\n    evaluator = DocREDEvaluator.from_train_file(train_file)\n    metrics = evaluator.evaluate(all_preds, gold)\n\n    n = len(selections)\n    n_ok = sum(1 for c in selections.values() if c.get(\"format_ok\", False))\n    metrics[\"n_docs\"] = n\n    metrics[\"n_predictions\"] = len(all_preds)\n    metrics[\"format_ok_rate\"] = n_ok / n if n else 0.0\n    return metrics\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Generation with checkpoint/resume support\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef load_generation_checkpoint(gen_file: Path) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Load previously generated candidates from JSONL (for resume).\"\"\"\n    docs = defaultdict(list)\n    if not gen_file.exists():\n        return {}\n    with open(gen_file) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            rec = json.loads(line)\n            docs[rec[\"doc_id\"]].append(rec)\n    logger.info(\"Resumed %d docs from %s\", len(docs), gen_file)\n    return dict(docs)\n\n\ndef run_generation(model, tokenizer, items, args, output_dir):\n    \"\"\"Generate N candidates per doc. Supports resume from prior run.\"\"\"\n    gen_file = output_dir / \"generations.jsonl\"\n    existing = load_generation_checkpoint(gen_file)\n\n    remaining = [it for it in items if it[\"doc_id\"] not in existing]\n    logger.info(\"Generation: %d total, %d done, %d remaining\",\n                len(items), len(existing), len(remaining))\n\n    if remaining:\n        with open(gen_file, \"a\") as fout:\n            for idx in tqdm(range(len(remaining)), desc=\"Generating\"):\n                item = remaining[idx]\n                outputs, trunc_flags = generate_batch(\n                    model, tokenizer, [item[\"prompt\"]],\n                    args.num_generations, args.temperature, args.max_new_tokens,\n                )\n                gens = outputs[0]\n                truncs = trunc_flags[0]\n\n                candidates = []\n                for gi, raw_text in enumerate(gens):\n                    parsed, fmt_ok = parse_model_output(raw_text)\n                    trunc = truncs[gi] if gi < len(truncs) else False\n                    rec = {\n                        \"doc_id\": item[\"doc_id\"],\n                        \"generation_idx\": gi,\n                        \"raw_text\": raw_text,\n                        \"parsed_triples\": parsed,\n                        \"format_ok\": fmt_ok,\n                        \"truncated\": trunc,\n                    }\n                    candidates.append(rec)\n                    fout.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n                existing[item[\"doc_id\"]] = candidates\n                fout.flush()\n\n    # Assemble per-doc dict keyed by doc_id from items (to attach gold/sents)\n    item_map = {it[\"doc_id\"]: it for it in items}\n    all_docs = {}\n    for doc_id, cands in existing.items():\n        if doc_id not in item_map:\n            continue\n        all_docs[doc_id] = {\n            \"candidates\": cands,\n            \"gold_triples\": item_map[doc_id][\"gold_triples\"],\n            \"sents\": item_map[doc_id][\"sents\"],\n        }\n    return all_docs\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"CED-as-reranker PoC\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--base_model\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--sft_adapter\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--data_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/ced_reranker_poc\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--nli_model\", default=\"cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_generations\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=0.7)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max_new_tokens\", type=int, default=2048)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--tau\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max_docs\", type=int, default=None)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # --- Step 1: Load model & generate ---\n    logger.info(\"Loading SFT model (merged, bf16, no quantize)...\")\n    model, tokenizer = load_model_and_tokenizer(\n        args.base_model, sft_adapter=args.sft_adapter, quantize=False,\n    )\n    # Qwen3 generation_config override\n    model.generation_config.temperature = args.temperature\n    model.generation_config.top_k = 0\n    model.generation_config.do_sample = True\n\n    logger.info(\"Preparing dev data...\")\n    items = prepare_generation_data(args.data_dir, \"dev\", tokenizer)\n    if args.max_docs:\n        items = items[:args.max_docs]\n    logger.info(\"Dev docs to process: %d\", len(items))\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    all_docs = run_generation(model, tokenizer, items, args, output_dir)\n    gen_time = time.time() - t0\n    logger.info(\"Generation done: %.1fs (%.2f s/doc)\", gen_time, gen_time / max(len(items), 1))\n\n    # Free LLM VRAM\n    del model, tokenizer\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # --- Step 2: CED scoring ---\n    logger.info(\"Loading NLI model for CED scoring...\")\n    ced_model = CEDRewardModel(model_name=args.nli_model, device=\"cuda:0\")\n\n    ced_sel, oracle_sel, rand_sel = {}, {}, {}\n    scores_file = output_dir / \"per_doc_scores.jsonl\"\n\n    # Correlation tracking\n    ced_list, f1_list = [], []\n\n    with open(scores_file, \"w\") as fout:\n        for doc_id, doc in tqdm(all_docs.items(), desc=\"CED scoring\"):\n            cands = doc[\"candidates\"]\n            gold_t = doc[\"gold_triples\"]\n            sents = doc[\"sents\"]\n\n            ced_scores = [\n                score_candidate_ced(c[\"parsed_triples\"], sents, ced_model, tau=args.tau)\n                for c in cands\n            ]\n            f1_scores = [\n                candidate_rel_f1(c[\"parsed_triples\"], gold_t)\n                for c in cands\n            ]\n\n            fout.write(json.dumps({\n                \"doc_id\": doc_id,\n                \"ced_scores\": [round(s, 4) for s in ced_scores],\n                \"oracle_f1_scores\": [round(s, 4) for s in f1_scores],\n            }, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n\n            # Track per-candidate pairs for correlation\n            ced_list.extend(ced_scores)\n            f1_list.extend(f1_scores)\n\n            # Strategy selections\n            ced_sel[doc_id] = cands[int(np.argmax(ced_scores))]\n            oracle_sel[doc_id] = cands[int(np.argmax(f1_scores))]\n            rand_sel[doc_id] = cands[random.randint(0, len(cands) - 1)]\n\n    del ced_model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # --- Step 3: Evaluate ---\n    logger.info(\"Evaluating strategies...\")\n\n    with open(Path(args.data_dir) / \"dev.json\") as f:\n        dev_data = json.load(f)\n    if args.max_docs:\n        keep = set(all_docs.keys())\n        dev_data = [d for d in dev_data if d.get(\"title\", \"\") in keep]\n    gold = gold_from_docred(dev_data)\n\n    train_file = str(Path(args.data_dir) / \"train_annotated.json\")\n    results = {}\n    for name, sel in [\n        (\"ced_reranker\", ced_sel),\n        (\"oracle_reranker\", oracle_sel),\n        (\"random_baseline\", rand_sel),\n    ]:\n        m = evaluate_strategy(sel, gold, train_file)\n        results[name] = m\n        logger.info(\"%s: f1=%.4f evi_f1=%.4f edcr=%.4f prec=%.4f rec=%.4f preds=%d\",\n                     name, m[\"f1\"], m[\"evi_f1\"], m.get(\"edcr\", 0),\n                     m[\"precision\"], m[\"recall\"], m[\"n_predictions\"])\n\n    # CED–F1 correlation (Spearman)\n    from scipy.stats import spearmanr\n    corr, pval = spearmanr(ced_list, f1_list)\n    results[\"ced_f1_correlation\"] = {\n        \"spearman_r\": round(corr, 4),\n        \"p_value\": float(f\"{pval:.2e}\"),\n        \"n_samples\": len(ced_list),\n    }\n    logger.info(\"CED–F1 Spearman r=%.4f (p=%s, n=%d)\", corr, f\"{pval:.2e}\", len(ced_list))\n\n    results[\"sft_baseline_reference\"] = {\n        \"f1\": 0.4057, \"evi_f1\": 0.7979, \"edcr\": 0.7118,\n        \"note\": \"greedy decode baseline\",\n    }\n    results[\"config\"] = vars(args)\n    results[\"generation_time_seconds\"] = round(gen_time, 1)\n\n    results_path = output_dir / \"results.json\"\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    logger.info(\"Results → %s\", results_path)\n\n    # Console summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 75)\n    print(f\"  CED-as-Reranker PoC  (N={args.num_generations}, T={args.temperature}, tau={args.tau})\")\n    print(\"=\" * 75)\n    print(f\"  {'Strategy':<20s} {'rel_f1':>8s} {'evi_f1':>8s} {'EDCR':>8s} \"\n          f\"{'prec':>8s} {'recall':>8s} {'preds':>6s}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 75)\n    for tag, label in [\n        (\"sft_baseline_reference\", \"SFT baseline\"),\n        (\"random_baseline\", \"Random\"),\n        (\"ced_reranker\", \"CED reranker\"),\n        (\"oracle_reranker\", \"Oracle (upper)\"),\n    ]:\n        m = results[tag]\n        if tag == \"sft_baseline_reference\":\n            print(f\"  {label:<20s} {m['f1']:>8.4f} {m['evi_f1']:>8.4f} \"\n                  f\"{m['edcr']:>8.4f} {'—':>8s} {'—':>8s} {'—':>6s}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {label:<20s} {m['f1']:>8.4f} {m['evi_f1']:>8.4f} \"\n                  f\"{m.get('edcr',0):>8.4f} {m['precision']:>8.4f} \"\n                  f\"{m['recall']:>8.4f} {m['n_predictions']:>6d}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 75)\n    c = results[\"ced_f1_correlation\"]\n    print(f\"  CED–F1 Spearman r = {c['spearman_r']:.4f}  (p = {c['p_value']})\")\n    print(\"=\" * 75)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"DONE: $?\"",
  "account": "root"
}
```
